- **cwe-sans-top25**: The CWE/SANS Top 25 is a curated list of the most prevalent and dangerous software weaknesses maintained by MITRE. Unlike OWASP (which focuses on web/API risks), CWE classifies root-cause weaknesses in software -- memory corruption, injection flaws, improper validation -- that lead to exploitable vulnerabilities.
